# Coverage: every promise has a proof (WORK-ORDER-12 section 5)

Joined by the main session on 2026-10-07 from the four auditors' reports (one for each area, written in their own worktrees, from main a66c137). One row for each numbered item of EVALS.md, each "Done means" of WORK-ORDER.md to WORK-ORDER-12.md, each test those documents name and each refusal of their registers.

**Kinds.** **PROVED** (a test or a self-test check, named in the row), **PERSON ONLY** (nothing but a person can see it; the row says why and whether it is in "De verificat de Dan"), **REPLACED** (a later work order or a decision of Dan's changed it). **There is no NO PROOF row in this file:** the auditors found 61 rows without a proof (their text said NO PROOF); the main session wrote the missing test for each in section 5 (the new tests are named in the row, under "Proof added in WORK-ORDER-12 section 5") or made it PERSON ONLY with the reason. The auditors' own counts, further down in each part, are those from before the tests were written.

**How a name is checked.** `Island.Tests` `CoverageTests.Every_Proof_Named_In_The_Coverage_Table_Exists` reads this file: a unit test named as `Class.Method` in a row must be in a test project, and a self-test check named as `check "text" (a stage file)` must have its text in that stage's source. A table that is not there passes.

## Counts after section 5

| part | rows the auditors called NO PROOF | now PROVED | now PERSON ONLY |
|---|---|---|---|
| capsule, movement, glasses, screens, modes | 23 | 20 | 3 |
| picks, pages, keys, scenes, settings, first start | 10 | 10 | 0 |
| icons, now playing, the pill, search, the add-on | 13 | 12 | 1 |
| the notice, the helpers, the Terminals page, speed, what is shipped | 31 | 31 | 0 |
| **total** | **77 rows** | **73** | **4** |

The four rows that became PERSON ONLY: E-X8 (the second-monitor step of the notice is not built: the owner decides), X-2 of the capsule part (placement across screens and scaling), X-7 (the blur follows the capsule: a look) and S-22 (the look of the pill's tile face). The rows that were PERSON ONLY, REPLACED or PROVED before are as the auditors wrote them.

Defects the tests of this section found and the main session repaired: `ship/store/privacy.md` did not name the moving light's field of `settings.json`; `ship/store/full-rights-justification.md` said the connection adds "two" hook entries to one helper's file; the new light's order of windows was not checked after a raise (it is now kept at every beat); a helper's refused write left a temporary file in the helper's own folder. The words for a broken pages, picks or scenes file are not shown (a log line only): a new text, so a proposal.

# Part 1. The capsule, its movement, the glasses, the screens, the modes

# Coverage table: the capsule, its movement, the glasses, the screens, the modes

Auditor "capsule" of WORK-ORDER-12.md §5 "Every promise has a proof". Written 2026-10-07 by Claude Sonnet 5.5 in the worktree `au-capsule` (branch `au-capsule`, from main `a66c137`). Nothing under `src/`, `tests/` or `extension/` was changed; nothing was started (no app, no self-test, no window, no capture).

**Area.** The shape and look of the capsule, the fly-in and fly-out, springs and fixed steps (EVALS M), the rim light and its three kinds (WO12 §2), the three glasses and Blur, the modes and what may appear over a game (EVALS X), which screen and scaling (EVALS W), the settings screen's ball motion (EVALS G), window placement and click-through, and the hidden/open cost.

**What a proof is here.** A unit test as `Project` `Class.Method` that I found in `tests/` (the name is checked against the sources by a script, see the end), or a self-test check as the exact text of its `report.Check("...")` in a `src\Island.App\*Stage.cs` file that I found. "The code does it" is not a proof; a test that would stay green when the promise broke is not a proof either (several rows say so).

**Kinds.** **PROVED**; **no proof (before section 5)** (with the smallest test that would prove it); **PERSON ONLY** (nothing but a person can see it; the row says why and whether STATE.md already lists it); **REPLACED** (a later work order or a decision changed it). One row has one kind. When a promise is partly proved and partly not, the row is **no proof (before section 5)** and its note says which part holds; a "Done means" row judges only its own sentence (verify green, the file exists, the named tests exist, the human item is listed), and the body promises of that section that have no proof are in part 5.

**The 284 tests of this area pass.** Run in this worktree: `dotnet test tests\Island.Tests --filter "<classes of this area>"` -> `Passed! Failed: 0, Passed: 284, Skipped: 0, Total: 284` (the filter is in the command list at the end). The self-test checks were not run (forbidden); their text was matched against the stage sources and the stage code that produces each was read for the checks I rely on.

## 4. Refusals of this area

| id | where it is written | the promise | kind | proof, by name | note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R-BLUR | WORK-ORDER-3.md §8, text 'BLUR_UNAVAILABLE' (three parts; the app uses Approved and says so once) | BLUR_UNAVAILABLE: what happened, why, what to do; shown once. | PROVED | `Island.Tests` `RefusalTests.Every_Refusal_Names_A_Next_Action`<br>`Island.Tests` `RefusalTests.No_Refusal_Looks_Like_A_Stack_Trace`<br>`Island.Tests` `GlassChoiceTests.Blur_That_Is_Saved_But_Not_Available_Draws_As_Approved_And_Stays_Saved`<br>`Island.Tests` `SettingsSessionTests.Blur_Is_Refused_When_It_Is_Not_Available_And_A_Glass_Choice_Is_Saved`<br>check in `BlurStage.cs`: `report.Check("the blur layer was created or its reason for being unavailable is a short code"...)` | Partial. The three parts and the fallback to Approved are proved. 'Says so once' (`IslandRuntime._blurFallbackSaid`, `AppHost` `BlurFellBack` -> `host.Refuse`) is not asserted anywhere; the BlurStage check is satisfied by any reason code (and returns early when blur is available). **Smallest test:** BlurStage / a short stage with a runtime whose `GlassLayer` is forced unavailable: call `SetGlass(Blur)` three times and assert the `BlurFellBack` event fired once, with a short code, and `rt.Glass == Approved`. **Proof added in WORK-ORDER-12 section 5:** check "when Blur is chosen and cannot be had the Approved glass is used and the reason is said once" (BlurStage.cs). |
| R-LIGHT | WORK-ORDER-12.md 'REFUSAL REGISTER — additions', LIGHT_UNAVAILABLE | LIGHT_UNAVAILABLE has its three parts; the setting shows Graphics card as not available with this reason. | **PROVED** | `Island.Tests` `RefusalTests.Every_Refusal_Names_A_Next_Action`<br>`Island.Tests` `RefusalTests.Every_Refusal_Names_A_Next_Action`<br>check in `SettingsStage.cs`: `report.Check("where the graphics-card light cannot be had it is shown as not available and refused with the refusal's words, and nothing is saved"...)` | The test is named for 'the words of the work order' but pins a shortened text (the order's is 275 characters, the balloon holds 255; recorded in STATE OWNER DECISIONS and pinned by `AttackTests`). The three parts are all there. |

## 5. Promises found in the bodies of the sections that have no proof (found while checking; not rows of the spec)

These are not counted in the totals of the table. Each says what the promise is, why nothing proves it, and the smallest test. Rows N-12.2.5 and X-L2 are the same promise.

| id | where it is written | the promise | kind | proof, by name | note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

## 6. What STATE.md's lists for the person do not contain yet

The PERSON ONLY rows are listed in STATE.md (the English NEEDS-HUMAN-VERIFY list and the Romanian "De verificat" section) except these four things (add them when the Romanian half is rewritten at the hand-over, WO12 §6):

1. **E-M7, second question:** press a page key while the island is still opening, and while it is leaving: does it change smoothly, without a jump?
2. **E-M8:** change Windows display scaling (100%, 125%, 150%) and restart the app: same shape, sharp edges? (Item 28 of the Romanian list asks only for a second screen.)
3. **X-L5:** the graphics-card light on a second screen with another scaling (does it follow the capsule, sharp?). The WO12 §2 item in the English list does not mention it.
4. **WO12 §2 "Moving light"** is in the English list (first) but not yet in the Romanian half; WO12 §6 step 3 says it must open with it.

## 7. Things I noticed that are not rows

- A test, `QuietTests.An_Unknown_Rate_Lets_Everything_Through_And_A_Clock_That_Goes_Back_Does_Not_Throw`, contains `Assert.True(budget.Admit(100, 16) || true)`, which can never fail (cosmetic).
- `RefusalTests.Every_Refusal_Names_A_Next_Action` is named for the work order's words but pins the shortened text (the order's text is 275 characters, a balloon holds 255). The shortening is recorded in STATE; the name is misleading.
- Three checks are silently absent (a NEEDS-HUMAN-VERIFY line is added instead) when their condition is not met: `QuietStage` "on the Media page in Do not disturb ..." when Windows gives no refresh rate; every check of `LightStage` after the first two when the compositor cannot be made; every check of `BlurStage` after the first when Blur is unavailable. A self-test run on a computer where that happens still reports all green. This is by design ("no verifier is not red") but it means those promises are proved only on a computer where the condition holds.
- `CostStage.LookAbovePercent` is 1.0 while WORK-ORDER-12.md §3 calls 1.12% "its highest reading on unchanged code"; the stage is the stricter of the two.
- The committed `review/selftest.json` is the output of the last `--cost` run (4 checks), not of a full self-test, as STATE already says.

Inventory of the tests: a throw-away Python script (kept outside the repository) walked `tests/**/*.cs`, and for each `[Fact]`/`[Theory]` recorded `Project|Class.Method` (1848 methods in 13 projects); the same script recorded the literal first argument of every `Check(` call in `src/**/*.cs` (395 literal checks; every `Check(` call in the stages has a literal or interpolated first argument). Every name and every check text cited in this table was matched against those inventories by a second script before the file was written (a name that is not found stops the script).
3. Reading: `Read` of `tests\Island.Tests\SpringTests.cs`, `IslandMachineTests.cs`, `ArcTests.cs`, `LookConstantsTests.cs`, `WindowMetricsTests.cs`, `RefusalTests.cs`, `Front\ModeTableTests.cs`, `Front\ShowDecisionTests.cs`, `Front\FrontReadingTests.cs`, `Front\NoticeFallbackTests.cs`, `Front\ModeMarkTests.cs`, `Front\PillTests.cs` (the one test), `Screens\ScreenChoiceTests.cs` (first half), `Speed\LightTests.cs`, `Speed\QuietTests.cs`, `SettingsEdit\GlassChoiceTests.cs`, `JointsTests.cs` (`GlassSettingTests`), and of `src\Island.App\MotionStage.cs`, `ModesStage.cs`, `BlurStage.cs`, `LightStage.cs`, `FrontStage.cs`, `ScreensStage.cs`, `CapsuleStage.cs` (first 140 lines), `SettingsScreen.cs` (the springs), `ScreenPlacer.cs`, `AgentNoticeHost.cs` (`Tick`), `IslandRuntime.cs` (`Show`, `RefreshPill`, `SetGlass`), `Core\WindowMetrics.cs`, `Core\Light\LightKind.cs`, `Core\SettingsEdit\LightChoice.cs`. Other tests are cited by name and by the first lines of their methods only.

# Part 2. Picks, pages, the + row, dragging, keys, adding by hand, scenes, settings and first start

# Coverage, area "picks": picks, pages, the + row, dragging, keys, adding by hand, scenes, the settings screen, the first start

Auditor: au-picks, WORK-ORDER-12 section 5. Written 2026-10-07 from branch `au-picks` (main HEAD `a66c137`). Only looked: no file under `src/`, `tests/`, `extension/` or in the
main folder was changed. Every row names its proof; a name that was not found by grep in `tests/` or in a `src\Island.App\*Stage.cs` does not appear as a proof.

## How to read this

- **Kind** is one of PROVED (a unit test or a self-test check that would go red if the promise broke, found by name), no proof (before section 5), PERSON ONLY, REPLACED.
- A unit test is written `Class.Method` and lives in `Island.Tests` (the only project that holds these tests; the Attack projects are not cited). Where one class name is used twice in
  `Island.Tests` the file is given. A self-test check is written as the stage file in square brackets followed by the quoted text, the text copied from its `report.Check("...")` (an escaped quote is left as `\"`).
- Where an old document names a test by a name that no longer exists but the same test exists under another class name, the row cites the real name and says so ("EVALS names
  `X.Y`; found as `Z.Y`"). The main session must keep that in mind for `CoverageTests`: the real name is the one that is in the code.
- A self-test check that reads "... or foregroundGranted: false" passes without proving anything when Windows refuses the foreground (it happens whenever Dan is using the laptop). Those rows
  are PROVED on the logic side (the unit tests) and the foreground half is listed in STATE.md "NEEDS-HUMAN-VERIFY" (cited in the row).
- A "Done means" row is PROVED when everything the section names (its tests, its self-test paragraph) exists and fits; what it leaves to a person is cited, not counted separately. A promise
  that is written inside a section but is not a Done means, a named test or an EVALS item is not a row: those are in "Further gaps" at the end (numbered G1...), each with its smallest test.
- Sections of the work orders that belong to the other three areas are not rows here (WO1 section 3 to 5 and 7, WO2 section 3, WO3 section 2/4/5, WO4 section 2, WO5 section 1 and 7, WO6 section 1/2/6,
  WO7 section 1 to 4, WO9, WO10 section 1, WO11 section 2 to 5, WO12 section 1/3). Borderline sections taken here: WO3 section 6 (Folders page), WO6 section 3 (start with Windows, a switch in Settings)
  and section 5 (the X button acts on a pick), WO8 SETTINGS_FROM_NEWER_VERSION (the settings file; the updates auditor has the rest of WO8 section 1).
- Test run of the audited tree: `dotnet test tests\Island.Tests` gave 1677 passed, 1 failed, 0 skipped. The failure is `ExtensionGuardTests.The_Zip_Holds_The_Addon_Without_Its_Tests_Its_Protocol_And_Its_Readme`
  (it compares the committed `ship/addon/island-addon.zip` with the `extension/` files byte for byte; the failure message shows 13 10 expected against 10 in the zip, i.e. this worktree's checkout turned the line endings of an `extension/` file into CRLF while the zip holds LF; most likely a worktree artifact and UNVERIFIED in the main folder): not in this area, and not a finding about the picks code.

## Most important gaps

1. **A broken `pages.json`, `picks.json` or `scenes.json` is never said out loud** (P7, SETTINGS_UNREADABLE): `AppHost.Start` writes a log line and nothing else, so a broken picks file shows an empty
   island with no word; only a broken `settings.json` (and a newer-version file) raises a notice, and no test or check covers even that path (also WO1 HOTKEY_TAKEN at start-up, WO8
   SETTINGS_FROM_NEWER_VERSION). One self-test check per file, reading `AppHost.LastRefusalCode`, closes the proof; the missing words are a likely defect for the main session to classify.
2. **"Run the setup again ... changes only what the person touches"** (WO7 section 6) is proved by a test whose key step is an empty loop; and **"the idle time does not run while a tile is lifted / the
   pointer is over the open second row / the wheel and arrows reset it"** (WO4/WO5, G2) has no test at all.
3. **Rows that are about what Settings shows** are only ever drawn, never read back: one row per action (K1), the island's key list (G3), the TERMINALS_PAGE_NO_ROOM line, the Scenes tray menu and its
   delete question (G4), and the rim of a page Dan made himself (P3). A walker over the built `SettingsView` (the stages already have `TextOfId` and `CountTextContaining`) would prove them together.

"The app says so": `AppHost.Start` shows SETTINGS_UNREADABLE only when `settings.json` is unreadable (and its text names `settings.json`); for `pages.json`, `picks.json` and `scenes.json` it writes one line in the log and shows nothing, so a person with a broken picks file sees an empty island and no word. | # | Where written | What is not proved | Smallest test |
|---|---|---|---|
| G1 | WORK-ORDER-2 §2 "While the island has the keyboard, Alt+F4 must not close its window." | `OverlayWindow.WndProc` swallows `WM_SYSKEYDOWN` (`handled = true`), which is what stops Alt+F4; nothing asserts it. | A check in `KeyboardStage`: send the island's window `WM_SYSKEYDOWN` with `VK_F4` and Alt held (a window message to the program's own window, not input) and assert the window is still loaded and `RawKey` was raised; or a source guard that `OverlayWindow` handles `WmSysKeyDown`. |
| G2 | WORK-ORDER-4 §1 / WORK-ORDER-5 §4, §5, §6 "The idle time does not run while ... a tile is lifted ... the pointer is over the open second row"; "the wheel and the arrows reset the idle clock". | `IslandController.OnRendering` (`IsLifted`, `PointerIsOver`) and `Wheel`/`ArrowClicked` (`Activity()`) have no test or check. | A check in `DragStage` with the runtime's idle at 2 s: lift a tile, wait 3.5 s, assert the island is still `Open`, cancel the drag and assert it leaves; the same with `PointerIsOver = () => true` and the second row open; and `c.Wheel(...)` moves `IdleDeadlineMs`. |
| G3 | WORK-ORDER-10 §2 "The settings screen gets, in its \"Key\" section, a short list of them, one line each." | `SettingsText.IslandKeys` is drawn by `KeySection`; no test reads it or the drawn screen. | Same walker as K1: `view.CountTextContaining(line) > 0` for each of `SettingsText.IslandKeys`. |
| G4 | WORK-ORDER-7 §5 "The tray menu gets \"Scenes\" with one entry per scene" and "delete after a yes/no question". | `TrayIcon.ScenesMenu` and `SettingsSession.AskDeleteScene` are never exercised. | A check in `ScenesStage` that builds the menu (`TrayIcon` with the scene list) and reads `DropDownItems` after `DropDownOpening`; a unit test `AskDeleteScene("scene-1")` returns a question naming the scene and `DeleteScene` without asking is not what the screen calls. |
| G5 | WORK-ORDER-3 §3 "A page with no picks shows a short capsule with its chip and the words \"Nothing here yet\"". | `PickItems.NothingHere` is never asserted (see P8). | See P8. |
| G6 | WORK-ORDER.md §6 "Register the six keybinds ... without auto-repeat." | `HotkeyHost` passes `ModNoRepeat`; nothing asserts it. | A source guard: the one `RegisterHotKey` call in `HotkeyHost.cs` ORs `Native.ModNoRepeat`. |
| G7 | WORK-ORDER-7 §6 "Run the setup again ... every step shows what is set **now** ... never puts a default over something the person had." | See the `Run_Again_...` row. | See that row. |

## Commands I ran

All run from `.worktrees\au-picks` (Git Bash, `export PATH="$PATH:/c/Program Files/dotnet"` for dotnet). Nothing under `src/`, `tests/`, `extension/` or the main folder was changed; no window, helper, terminal or real settings folder was touched.

```
git log --oneline | head -3 ; git status --short ; ls tests src src/Island.App
python ext.py . tests.txt          # scratch script: lists every [Fact]/[Theory] as Project|Class.Method|file (1842 methods)
python chk.py . checks.txt         # scratch script: lists every report.Check("...") text of src/Island.App/*.cs (395 checks)
python named.py                    # scratch script: every Class.Method named in EVALS.md and WORK-ORDER*.md, FOUND or MISSING
grep -rn ... src tests             # many: HotkeyHost, OverlayWindow, AppHost, IslandController, TrayIcon, GuardTests, OutsideGuardTests, FirstStart, StarterPicks
dotnet test "tests/Island.Tests" --nologo -v q
python verify.py                   # scratch script: every cited Class.Method must be in tests/ and every cited stage check text in its stage file
```

`dotnet test tests\Island.Tests`: Failed 1, Passed 1677, Skipped 0, Total 1678 (44 s). The one failure is `ExtensionGuardTests.The_Zip_Holds_The_Addon_Without_Its_Tests_Its_Protocol_And_Its_Readme`
(zip bytes against `extension/` bytes: line endings 13 10 against 10 in this worktree). Every unit test cited above is in the 1677 that passed. `verify.py` finds every cited `Class.Method` in `tests/` (the ones that appear
in plain text, not in code spans, are the old names that no longer exist) and every cited stage check text in the stage's source. The scratch scripts are in the session's scratchpad, not in the repository.

# Part 3. Icons, now playing, the pill, search, the add-on and its tabs

# Coverage audit, area "icons" (WORK-ORDER-12 §5): icons, now playing, the pill, search, the add-on and its tabs

Auditor: icons. Worktree `au-icons` (branch `au-icons`, from main `a66c137`). Read-only: no file under `src/`, `tests/`, `extension/` was changed; no window, helper, node or screen capture was used.
Invented names only. Written 2026-10-07.

## 0. How to read this

Kinds, in the third column of every table:

- **PROVED**: a unit test or self-test check I FOUND in the worktree, and that would go red if the promise broke.
- **PROVED+GAP**: the core of the promise is proved by what is named; a named part of it is not. The part is in the last column, with the smallest test that would prove it.
- **no proof (before section 5)**: nothing found that goes red when the promise breaks (or the named test is missing, or tests a function the app never calls). The last column holds the smallest test.
- **PERSON ONLY**: only a person can see it; the last column says whether it is already in STATE.md "De verificat de Dan" (RO half, "Ce era deja" = the numbered list, X1-X3 = add-on, K1-K5 = WO10, N1-N13 = WO7).
- **REPLACED**: a later work order or a decision of Dan's changed it; the last column names which.

Names: unit tests as `Project` + `Class.Method` (project is `Island.Tests` unless said); self-test checks as the exact text of the `report.Check("...")` literal in the `src\Island.App\*Stage.cs` file named (where the source literal has `\"` it is repeated here; `{i + 1}` is source text); add-on tests as file + test title in `extension/tests` (I did NOT run node: the add-on titles are found by grep only).

Things the documents say that the code does not match (reading notes):

1. EVALS I3 and WO3 §2 name `GuardTests.App_Has_No_Internet_Client`; the test is `OutsideGuardTests.App_Has_No_Internet_Client`. A `CoverageTests` that looks for the class name written in the documents will not find it.
2. EVALS I8 names `IconStoreTests.Only_Icons_Of_Picks_Are_Saved`: no such test, no `IconStore` anywhere in the repository.
3. `IconChoice.Choose` (and `IconPick.Undersized`, `IconPick.UseLetters`) are called by no code in `src/` or `tools/`; only `IconChoice.TwoLetterMark` and `IconChoice.IsUsable` are live. Three EVALS tests (I4, I5, I9) test the dead function. See F-3.
4. The pretend-add-on checks are written as `"pretend add-on: " + step.Name` in `TabsStage.cs`; the step names are in `src\Island.Bridge\PretendScript.cs`, not in any `*Stage.cs`. A check that looks for the whole text in a stage's source will not find them (cite the prefix and the step name).
5. `SearchStage.cs` has `\"tu\" shows its two matches and Google's tile, Tunes selected`: WO7 §3 says "two tiles"; Dan's later request added the Google tile (STATE.md BASELINE — WO11, "the Google search tile").
6. EVALS section L (looks decided 6 Oct) is a set of numbered items that no auditor's list of letters names. I took L1, L2, L3, L7, L8, L9 (they are about this area) as extra rows; L4, L5, L6, L10, L11 are for the other auditors.
7. In this worktree `ExtensionGuardTests.The_Zip_Holds_The_Addon_Without_Its_Tests_Its_Protocol_And_Its_Readme` is RED: `git config core.autocrlf` is `true` here, the working copy of `extension/` is CRLF, the zip holds LF files, the test compares bytes. In the main folder the files are LF and it passes. See F-4.
8. Work orders with no section of this area: WORK-ORDER.md (placeholder items only: "Clicking an item selects it", "controls do nothing" are REPLACED by WO3 §5/WO5/WO7 §2), WORK-ORDER-2.md (keys), WORK-ORDER-6.md (its §5 close button is the capsule/picks auditor's; only its tab-close protocol message is named here), WORK-ORDER-11.md (Terminals page; its tiles take the round face of WO10 §1, proved in rows C-RI*), WORK-ORDER-12.md (§1-§4, §6 are other areas).
9. Strictness applied: "the code does it" was not accepted. A test that exercises a function the app does not call was not accepted. A self-test check that passes only because the grant of the keyboard is pretended was not accepted (F4).

## 5. Findings (the three biggest first; each with the evidence and the smallest repair; none changes a file here)

- **F-1 (MEDIUM, from reading code; not reproduced, I may not start the app)**: `IconCache.Fetch` (`src/Island.App/PickPages.cs`) stores the result `_icons[pick.Id] = icon` even when it is `null`, and `Get` never asks again. `ProgramIcons.ProgramIcon` knows a miss must not be cached ("a miss is not cached: the catalog may not have been read yet"), but the catalog's `Loaded` event is wired to nothing in `AppHost`/`AppWorld`, and the catalog is awaited only on a first start (`StartersInstalled`). The first `ItemsOf` happens at `IslandMachine` construction (`_capsuleTargetWidth = CapsuleLayout.SizeFor(Contents)` calls `itemsOf` for page one), right after `RealWorld.Create` has started the catalog's first read. A program that only the catalog can locate (anything not under the Windows folders) then keeps its letters until the next start. Dan reported icons working, so the race is usually won; the repair is cheap: do not remember a `null` (or forget nulls when the catalog's `Loaded` fires). Proof test: a self-test check with a pretend icon source that returns `null` on the first call and an icon on the second: after `world.Changed` the tile has the icon.
- **F-2 (LOW)**: `PickPages.TabIconOf` caches decoded tab icons under `tab.Key:host:png.Length`; a changed icon of the same length on the same host keeps the old picture (EVALS I7 "never on the wrong item"). Repair: key by reference of the `byte[]` or by a hash.
- **F-3 (LOW, safe improvement)**: `IconChoice.Choose`/`IconPick` are dead; three EVALS tests prove only them. Either wire it (then I4/I5/I9 and the "flagged in the log" half of I5 become real) or delete it and re-point I4, I5, I9 to the live checks. A promise nobody built: I5 "flagged in the log" (nothing logs an undersized icon).
- **F-4 (LOW)**: `ExtensionGuardTests.The_Zip_Holds_The_Addon_Without_Its_Tests_Its_Protocol_And_Its_Readme` compares the zip's bytes with the working copy and is red on any CRLF checkout (this worktree). Repair: a `.gitattributes` line `extension/** text eol=lf`, or compare after normalising line endings.
- **F-5 (MEDIUM for "a feature that does nothing and nothing says so")**: the pill's five handlers have no proof (A-N9): `PillView.RaiseForSelfTest` is dead. Repair is a test, not code.

# Part 4. The notice, the helpers' connections, the Terminals page, the cost and speed runs, what is shipped, updates

# Coverage audit, area NOTICE (WORK-ORDER-12 section 5)

Area: the notice ("Your agent is done"), the helpers' connections (Claude Code, Codex; Gemini and Antigravity are BLOCKED), the Terminals page with its rings and sessions, the cost and speed runs, what is shipped (`ship/`), updates. EVALS sections A, U, D; and from WORK-ORDER.md to WORK-ORDER-12.md every "Done means", named test, named self-test check and refusal that belongs here.

Auditor: au-notice, branch `au-notice`, from main `a66c137`. Read-only: nothing under `src/`, `tests/`, `extension/` or the main folder was changed. The island, the self-test, every window and every helper were left alone.

## How to read this

- **Kind** is one of: `proved` (a test or a self-test check that goes red if the promise breaks), `no proof (before section 5)`, `PERSON ONLY`, `REPLACED`.
- A unit test is written as the project name in backticks, a slash, then the class and method in backticks (for example the project `Island.Tests` and the test `NoticeTests.A_Newer_Notice_Replaces_The_Older`), as found by grep in `tests/`. A self-test check is written as the exact text of its `report.Check("...")`, found in a `src\Island.App\*Stage.cs` file, in backticks. Where a check's text holds `\"` it is copied as the source has it.
- "Where written" gives the file and an anchor (a heading or a verbatim phrase), never a line number.
- A name that the work order gives and that I did not find is not a proof. Where the same promise is proved under another name, the row says so and names the proof I found.
- Strictness: "the code does it" is not a proof. A test that exists but pins something other than the promise is `no proof (before section 5)` with the reason.
- For every `no proof (before section 5)` row the last column holds the smallest test that would prove it.
- Rows are one per item. Sub-promises found inside prose, which the documents do not name as a test, are in the appendix "Further unproved promises noticed" (not counted).

## What I ran (details at the end)

- `dotnet test tests\Island.Tests` in this worktree: 1678 tests, 1677 passed, **1 failed**: `ExtensionGuardTests.The_Zip_Holds_The_Addon_Without_Its_Tests_Its_Protocol_And_Its_Readme`. The cause is the line endings of this checkout (`core.autocrlf=true`: the files of `extension/` are CRLF in the worktree and LF inside the committed zip), not the add-on: see finding N-1. It passes where the files are LF (the main folder; STATE.md records the project green).
- `node --test` in `extension/`: 115 passed, 0 failed (the add-on's own tests; they start no browser).
- Not run on purpose: `Island.Agents.Tests` (its tests start `Island.Notify` as a process) and every `Island.Attack*` / `Island.Redteam.*` project (not needed to confirm a name). Names from those projects are cited as found by grep.
- The method for names: all 1850 `public void/Task` methods of `tests/` were indexed (class, method, project, file), and every name in the work orders that ends in `Tests.<Method>` was looked up in that index (386 names; 28 not found, of which the ones in this area are listed under the rows). For the named tests of this area I counted the assertions in each body (all have three or more) and read about thirty bodies in full.

## Findings first (the audit's output for the main session)

Each is stated as evidence plus the smallest repair; the class (defect, safe improvement, proposal) is the main session's call.

The committed zip holds LF text, the checkout holds CRLF. | MEDIUM (a fresh clone on Windows is red for a reason that is not a defect of the add-on; and the byte comparison is the only proof that `ship/addon/island-addon.zip` is current) | Compare text entries after normalising CRLF to LF, or add `.gitattributes` (`extension/** text eol=lf`). | N-17 | `src/Island.App/CostStage.cs` | The hidden-cost run flags NEEDS-HUMAN-VERIFY above `LookAbovePercent = 1.0`; WORK-ORDER-11 section 4 and WORK-ORDER-12 section 3 say "above its highest reading on unchanged code, 1.12%". The code is stricter than the text (a reading of 1.05% sets the flag in the code, and would not under the text). | LOW | Align the text or the constant; record which. |
| N-18 | `WORK-ORDER-8.md` section 3 ("The tray's open settings file. They do not: the six methods of `PackagedChoiceTests` never mention the tray; the behaviour is `AppHost.OpenSettingsFile` (`if (_package.IsPackaged) OpenSettingsScreen();`), unreached by any test or check. | LOW | A test on a small pure function `TrayChoice.OpenSettingsFile(isPackaged)` returning `Screen` or `File`, used by `AppHost`. |

## 3.8 WORK-ORDER-11 section 5: Codex, Antigravity's terminal program, Gemini

The order names its tests once with `<H>` for each helper. Codex is built. Gemini and Antigravity are BLOCKED by the order's own rule ("a helper that has no way to run a hook without waiting for it is BLOCKED"); their hooks pages (read 2026-10-07) say the helper waits for every hook. ## 7. The commands I ran

All in `.worktrees\au-notice`, Git Bash, with `export PATH="$PATH:/c/Program Files/dotnet"` where `dotnet` was used. Nothing was started from `dist/`, `src/Island.App/bin` was not touched, no window was shown, no helper was started.

```
git log --oneline | head -3
git ls-files --eol extension/background.js extension/manifest.json ship/addon/island-addon.zip
git config core.autocrlf
git log --oneline -- tests/Island.Tests/Fixtures/v1
git check-ignore -v ship/app ship/Island.msix
dotnet test ".worktrees\au-notice\tests\Island.Tests" --nologo -v q
   -> Failed: 1, Passed: 1677, Total: 1678 (the one failure is ExtensionGuardTests.The_Zip_Holds_..., N-1)
dotnet test tests/Island.Tests --no-build --nologo --filter "FullyQualifiedName~The_Zip_Holds_The_Addon"
   -> reproduced the failure alone: bytes 13,10 expected, 10 found
cd extension && node --test
   -> tests 115, pass 115, fail 0
grep -rn "SecondScreenWithoutFullscreen" src tests --include=*.cs
grep -rn "NOTIFY_MISSING\|AGENT_SETTINGS_NOT_ALLOWED\|HOOKS_FILE_UNREADABLE\|LIGHT_UNAVAILABLE\|SETTINGS_FROM_NEWER_VERSION\|ALREADY_RUNNING\|TERMINALS_PAGE_NO_ROOM" src tests --include=*.cs
grep -rn "NoticeClicked\|RaiseClickForSelfTest\|TerminalChoice.Choose" tests src --include=*.cs
grep -rn "NoTerminal\|PageFillsItself" src tests --include=*.cs
grep -rn "ClientAreaAnimation" src tests --include=*.cs
grep -rn "backup\|BackupName\|\.bak" tests --include=*.cs
grep -rln "\.claude\|\.codex\|\.gemini\|\.agents" tests src --include=*.cs
grep -rn "PackageReference\|CopyFromScreen\|PrintWindow\|NuGet" tests/Island.Tests/*.cs
grep -a -n "public void" tests/Island.Tests/ShipGuardTests.cs
grep -n -i "terminal\|codex\|gemini\|antigravity\|light\|helper\|agent" ship/store/listing-en.md
sed -n 45,58p ship/store/full-rights-justification.md
cat ship/store/privacy.md
ls review ship ship/addon ship/package ship/store
```

Index scripts (in the session's scratchpad, not in the repository): an indexer of every `public void|Task` method of `tests/**/*.cs` with its innermost class and project (1850 methods), a lookup of every `...Tests.<Method>` name found in EVALS.md and WORK-ORDER.md to WORK-ORDER-12.md (386 names), an assertion counter, and a checker that every backticked self-test check text in this report is the exact text of a `.Check("...")` in a `src\Island.App\*Stage.cs` file (395 literals indexed; every cited text was found, except the interpolated one cited by its prefix).

What I could not check: the behaviour of the real `OutsideAgentConnector` / `OutsideCodexConnector` files (by design nothing can aim them), `Island.Agents.Tests` and the `Island.Attack*` projects (not run), the self-test itself (forbidden), any picture of the moving light (impossible), `ship/app/` and the published self-test (not in git), whether the add-on works in a real browser, whether a real Claude Code or Codex accepts what Connect writes.
